00005: # y388; the reference upright is nearer x150 and ends around y350. Its bedding
00006: # also occupies too much foreground. Column and far equipment remain anchors.
00007: # Hypothesis: the berth extends too far toward the camera. Revise only its
00008: # longitudinal dimensions and attached parts; retain camera and other assets.
00009: #
00010: # Evidence/ownership ledger, approximate reference pixel bounds:

00041:

00318: def owner_meshes(slot):
00319:     root = bpy.data.objects.get(slot)
00320:     if root is None or root.type != 'EMPTY':
00321:         candidates = [o for o in bpy.data.objects
00322:                       if o.type == 'EMPTY' and o.name.split('.')[0] == slot]
00323:         assert candidates, f"Missing semantic marker: {slot}"
00324:         root = candidates[0]
00325:     meshes = [o for o in root.children_recursive if o.type == 'MESH']
00326:     assert meshes, f"No mesh descendants: {slot}"
00327:     return meshes
00328: 
00329: def customize(slot,metallic=None,roughness=None,texture=None):
00330:     for obj in owner_meshes(slot):
00331:         obj.data = obj.data.copy()
00332:         for idx,original in enumerate(list(obj.data.materials)):
00333:             if original is None:
00334:                 continue
00335:             mat = original.copy()
00336:             obj.data.materials[idx] = mat
00337:             mat.use_nodes = True
00338:             nodes,links = mat.node_tree.nodes,mat.node_tree.links
00339:             bs = next((n for n in nodes if n.type == 'BSDF_PRINCIPLED'),None)
00340:             if bs is None:
00341:                 continue
00342:             if metallic is not None:
00343:                 bs.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = metallic
00344:             if roughness is not None:
00345:                 bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = roughness
00346:             if texture == "stone":
00347:                 noise = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00348:                 noise.inputs["Scale"].default_value = 185
00349:                 noise.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 2.5
00350:                 ramp = nodes.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
00351:                 ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position = .22
00352:                 ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = (.065,.061,.047,1)
00353:                 ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position = .77
00354:                 ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = (.57,.53,.42,1)
00355:                 middle = ramp.color_ramp.elements.new(.51)
00356:                 middle.color = (.255,.24,.19,1)
00357:                 links.new(noise.outputs["Fac"],ramp.inputs[0])
00358:                 links.new(ramp.outputs["Color"],bs.inputs["Base Color"])
00359:                 bump = nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00360:                 bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = .25
00361:                 bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .006
00362:                 links.new(noise.outputs["Fac"],bump.inputs["Height"])
00363:                 links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"],bs.inputs["Normal"])
00364:             elif texture == "fabric":
00365:                 noise = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00366:                 noise.inputs["Scale"].default_value = 220
00367:                 noise.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 2
00368:                 bump = nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00369:                 bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = .20
00370:                 bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .002
00371:                 links.new(noise.outputs["Fac"],bump.inputs["Height"])
00372:                 links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"],bs.inputs["Normal"])

00042: def add(parent, name, node):
00043:     return parent.include(name, node)

00045: def ellipsoid(parent, name, center, scale, color, opacity=1):
00046:     node = ball(1, center=center, color=color, opacity=opacity)
00047:     node.resize(scale, pivot=center)
00048:     return add(parent, name, node)

00050: def tube(parent, name, points, radius, color):
00051:     return add(parent, name, sweep_tube(points=points, radius=radius, color=color))

00300: def owner_meshes(slot):
00301:     root = bpy.data.objects.get(slot)
00302:     if root is None or root.type != 'EMPTY':
00303:         candidates = [
00304:             o for o in bpy.data.objects
00305:             if o.type == 'EMPTY' and o.name.split('.')[0] == slot
00306:         ]
00307:         assert candidates, f"Missing semantic marker: {slot}"
00308:         root = candidates[0]
00309:     meshes = [o for o in root.children_recursive if o.type == 'MESH']
00310:     assert meshes, f"No mesh descendants: {slot}"
00311:     return meshes
00312: 
00313: def customize(slot, metallic=None, roughness=None, texture=None):
00314:     for obj in owner_meshes(slot):
00315:         obj.data = obj.data.copy()
00316:         for idx, original in enumerate(list(obj.data.materials)):
00317:             if original is None:
00318:                 continue
00319:             mat = original.copy()
00320:             obj.data.materials[idx] = mat
00321:             mat.use_nodes = True
00322:             nodes = mat.node_tree.nodes
00323:             links = mat.node_tree.links
00324:             bs = next((n for n in nodes if n.type == 'BSDF_PRINCIPLED'), None)
00325:             if bs is None:
00326:                 continue
00327:             if metallic is not None:
00328:                 bs.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = metallic
00329:             if roughness is not None:
00330:                 bs.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = roughness
00331:             if texture == "stone":
00332:                 noise = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00333:                 noise.inputs["Scale"].default_value = 185
00334:                 noise.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 2.5
00335:                 ramp = nodes.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
00336:                 ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position = .22
00337:                 ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = (.065,.061,.047,1)
00338:                 ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position = .77
00339:                 ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = (.57,.53,.42,1)
00340:                 middle = ramp.color_ramp.elements.new(.51)
00341:                 middle.color = (.255,.24,.19,1)
00342:                 links.new(noise.outputs["Fac"], ramp.inputs[0])
00343:                 links.new(ramp.outputs["Color"], bs.inputs["Base Color"])
00344:                 bump = nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00345:                 bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = .25
00346:                 bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .006
00347:                 links.new(noise.outputs["Fac"], bump.inputs["Height"])
00348:                 links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"], bs.inputs["Normal"])
00349:             elif texture == "fabric":
00350:                 noise = nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexNoise")
00351:                 noise.inputs["Scale"].default_value = 220
00352:                 noise.inputs["Detail"].default_value = 2
00353:                 bump = nodes.new("ShaderNodeBump")
00354:                 bump.inputs["Strength"].default_value = .20
00355:                 bump.inputs["Distance"].default_value = .002
00356:                 links.new(noise.outputs["Fac"], bump.inputs["Height"])
00357:                 links.new(bump.outputs["Normal"], bs.inputs["Normal"])
00358: 
00359: customize("stone_slab", roughness=.62, texture="stone")
00360: customize("backsplash", roughness=.62, texture="stone")
00361: customize("towel_ring", metallic=.82, roughness=.23)
00362: customize("sink_and_faucet", metallic=.70, roughness=.27)
